# Scope and methodology

This attribution covers packages that leave Recurly's infrastructure and are loaded or run on merchant or end-user browsers and devices. Scope is bundle-truth for recurly-app (snappy) and platform-ui via the webpack build module graph, and production-scoped npm dependency closures for the remaining library repositories. Full transitive scope is included — both direct and indirect dependencies in the browser bundle.

**Repositories covered (8 of 8):** recurly-js, checkout-ui, recurly-hosted-field, pay-with-amazon, RevRec-Client, platform-ui, recurly-app (snappy/), and react-recurly.

  <tr><td>With full license text</td><td>338</td></tr>
  <tr><td>Needs manual review</td><td>27</td></tr>
  <tr><td>Legal review required</td><td>29</td></tr>
</table>

<div className="rp-callout rp-callout-important">
  <div><strong><i className="fa-solid fa-circle-exclamation" aria-hidden="true"></i> Review before publishing</strong>48 packages are flagged for review in the section below. Each one is marked with an icon in its accordion and opens with the specific reason it needs attention. Clear these with Legal or Engineering before distributing this notice externally.</div>
</div>

# Packages requiring review

Every package in this section carries a verification comment from the attribution scan. The accordion icon signals the type of concern, and each entry opens with the exact reason it was flagged.

## Commercial licenses — not open source

These are proprietary or paid-license packages, not standard open source. Confirm Recurly holds a valid commercial license before distributing, and do not list them as standard OSS attributions.

## First-party Recurly code

These packages are Recurly's own code, pulled in as dependencies and auto-flagged as if they were third-party OSS. Confirm the categorization — their own transitive dependencies are already attributed elsewhere in this notice.

<Accordion title="@recurly/platform-ui v1.25.1" icon="fa-solid fa-building">
  **Review reason:** First-party Recurly UI library, a direct dependency of snappy — not third-party OSS; its own dependencies are already fully attributed under the platform-ui repo's own scan in this project. Do not treat the 'no LICENSE file' auto-flag on this entry as a real third-party gap.

  **License:** UNKNOWN · **Used in:** recurly-app (snappy/)

  **Source:** <a href="https://github.com/recurly/platform-ui" target="_blank">[https://github.com/recurly/platform-ui](https://github.com/recurly/platform-ui)</a>

  _No license text is included in the source package._
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="@recurly/react-recurly v2.0.1" icon="fa-solid fa-building">

<Accordion title="world-countries v1.8.1" icon="fa-solid fa-code-branch">
  **Review reason:** Open Database License (ODbL 1.0) — carries attribution AND share-alike obligations on derivative databases, unlike a plain permissive license. The reproduced text is only the ODbL/DbCL reference URLs, not the full license body. Needs legal review before publication, especially if country data is redistributed or transformed.
